====================================================================================================
QUESTION: If the employee becomes totally disabled before age 60 and remains disabled for 6 months, what happens to voluntary life insurance premiums?
CATEGORY: CAT3_CONDITIONAL_REASONING
EXPECTED ANSWER: The voluntary life insurance premiums will be waived.
EVIDENCE: 11.1 If you become totally disabled before age 60 and remain disabled for 6 consecutive months, your voluntary life insurance premiums will be waived.
====================================================================================================
